Listado de tablas existentes ordenadas alfabéticamente

- Vistas

## 4.-Vistas



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Vista</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Tablas</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Campos relacionados</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Consulta de creación de vista</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>t_adquis</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>adquis</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>ADQUIS.aanadq = ADQDET.adnadq</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT adquis.*,adqdet.*, titulo.*,provee.*, adqlec.*, lector.*,cuenta.*,sucurs.*, moneda.* FROM (((((ADQUIS INNER JOIN ADQDET ON ADQUIS.aanadq = ADQDET.adnadq) INNER JOIN TITULO ON ADQDET.adnit = TITULO.tinit)) INNER JOIN PROVEE ON ADQUIS.aanprv = PROVEE.pvnprv) LEFT JOIN CUENTA ON ADQUIS.aacocu = CUENTA.cucocu ADQUIS.acocu = SUCURS.sucosu)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT ADQUIS.*,adqdet.*, titulo.*,provee.*, adqlec.*, lector.*,cuenta.*,sucurs.*,moneda.* FROM (((((ADQUIS INNER JOIN ADQDET ON ADQUIS.aanadq = ADQDET.adnadq) INNER JOIN TITULO ON ADQDET.adnit = TITULO.tinit)) INNER JOIN PROVEE ON ADQUIS.aanprv = PROVEE.pvnprv) LEFT JOIN CUENTA ON ADQUIS.aacocu = CUENTA.cucocu) LEFT JOIN ADQLET ON ADQLET.adnseq = ADQLEC.aladsq) LEFT JOIN LECTOR ON ADQLEC.alnlec = LECTOR.lenlec) LEFT JOIN MONEDA ON ADQUIS.aacomo = MONEDA.mocomo) LEFT JOIN SUCURS ON ADQUIS.aacosu = SUCURS.sucosu)</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>t_adqdet</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>adqdet</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>ADQDET.adnadq = ADQUIS.aanadq</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT ADQUIS.*,adqdet.*,adquis.*,titulo.*,provee.*,adqlec.*,lector.*,cuenta.* FROM (((((ADQDET LEFT JOIN ADQUIS ON ADQDET.adadq = ADQUIS.aanprv = PROVEE.pvnprv) LEFT JOIN ADQLEC ON ADQDET.adnseq = ADQLEC.aladsq) LEFT JOIN LECTOR ON ADQLEC.alnlec = LECTOR.lenlec) LEFT JOIN PROVEE ON ADQUIS.aanprv)</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT ADQUIS.*,adqdet.*,adquis.*,titulo.*,provee.*,adqulec.*,lector.*,cuenta.* FROM (((((ADQDET LEFT JOIN ADQUIS ON ADQUIS.aanadq = ADQUIS.aanadq) LEFT JOIN ADQLEC ON ADQUIS.aaocou = CUENTA.cucocu) LEFT JOIN TITULO ON ADQDET.adnseq = ADQUIS.aaocou)</td></tr></table>

word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT copias.*, copnot.*, biblio.*, sucurs.*, coploc.*, coptip.*, copest.*, proced.*, copco1.* FROM (((((copias LEFT JOIN copnot ON copias.cobarc = copnot.cnbarc) LEFT JOIN proced ON copias.cocopr = proced.pccopr) LEFT JOIN sucurs ON copias.cocosu = sucurs.sucosu) LEFT JOIN biblio ON sucurs.sucobi = biblio.bicobi) LEFT JOIN coploc ON copias.cococl = coploc.clcoccl) LEFT JOIN coptip ON copias.cococp = coptip.cpcocp) LEFT JOIN copest ON copias.cococo = copost.cecoce) LEFT JOIN copco1 ON copias.cococ1 = copco1.c1coc1</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>t_factur</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>factur\nfacdet\nmoneda\ncuenta\nproveetitulo</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>factur.ffnseq = facdet.fddfsq\nfactur.ffcomo = moneda.mocomo\nfacdet.fdcocu = cuenta.cucoccu\nfactur.ffnprv = provee.pvnprv\nfacdet.fdntit = titulo.tinit\nfactur.ffcobi = biblio.bicobi\nfacdet.fdcosu = sucurs.sucosu</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT factur.*, facdet.*, moneda.*, cuenta.*, provee.*, titulo.*, biblio.*, sucurs.* FROM (((((factur INNER JOIN facdet ON factur.ffnseq = facdet.fddfsq) INNER JOIN moneda ON factur.ffcomo = moneda.mocomo) INNER JOIN cuenta ON facdet.fdcocu = cuenta.cucocu) INNER JOIN provee ON factur.ffnprv = provee.pvnprv) LEFT JOIN titulo ON facdet.fdntit = titulo.tinit) LEFT JOIN biblio ON factur.ffcobi = biblio.bicobi) LEFT JOIN sucurs ON facdet.fdcosu = sucurs.sucosu</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>t_facet</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>facet factur moneda cuenta provee titulo biblio sucurs</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>factur.ffnseq = facet.fddfsq factur.ffcomo = moneda.mocomo facet.fdcocu = cuenta.cucocu factur.ffnprv = provee.pvnprv facet.fdntit = titulo.tinit factur.ffcobi = biblio.bicobi facet.fdcosu = sucurs.sucosu</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT facet.*, factur.*, moneda.*, cuenta.*, provee.*, titulo.*, biblio.*, sucurs.* FROM (((((factur INNER JOIN facet ON factur.ffnseq = facet.fddfsq) INNER JOIN moneda ON factur.ffcomo = moneda.mocomo) INNER JOIN cuenta ON facdet.fdcocu = cuenta.cucocu) INNER JOIN provee ON factur.ffnprv = provee.pvnprv) LEFT JOIN titulo ON facdet.fdntit = titulo.tinit) LEFT JOIN biblio ON factur.ffcobi = biblio.bicobi) LEFT JOIN sucurs ON facdet.fdcosu = sucurs.sucosu</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>t_lecdsi</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>lecdsi lector lectip lecco1 lecco2 lecco3</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>lecco1.lcol1 = lector.lecol1 lecco2.lcol2 = lector.lecol2 lecco3.lcol3 = lector.lecol3 lectip.lpcolp = lector.lecolp lecdsi.ldnlec = lector.lenlec</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT lecdsi.*, lector.*, lectip.*, lecco1.*, lecco2.*, lecco3.* FROM LECDSI LEFT JOIN (LECTIP RIGHT JOIN (LECO3 RIGHT JOIN (LECO2 RIGHT JOIN (LECO1 RIGHT JOIN LECTOR ON LECO1.lcol1 = LECTOR.lecol1) ON LECCO2.lcol2 = LECTOR.lecol2) ON LECCO3.lcol3 = LECTOR.lecol3) ON LECTIP.lpcolp = LECTOR.lecolp) ON LECDSI.ldnlec = LECTOR.lenlec</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>t_lector</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>lector biblio sucurs lectip lecco1 lecco2 lecco3 codprf</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>codprf.cfcocf=lector.lecocf lecco1.lcol1 = lector.lecol1 lecco2.lcol2 = lector.lecol2 lecco3.lcol3 = lector.lecol3 lectip.lpcolp=lector.lecolp biblio.bicobi=lector.lecolbi</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT lector.*, biblio.*, sucurs.*, lectip.*, lecco1.*, lecco2.*, lecco3.*, codprf.* FROM biblio RIGHT JOIN (sucurs RIGHT JOIN (lectip RIGHT JOIN (lecco3 RIGHT JOIN (lecco2 RIGHT JOIN (lecco1 RIGHT JOIN (codprf RIGHT JOIN LECTOR ON lcco1.lcol1=lector.lecol1) ON lecco2.lcol2= lector.lecol2) ON lecco3.lcol3=lector.lecol3) ON lectip.lpcolp=lector.lecolp) ON sucurs.sucosu=lector.lecosu ON biblio.bicobi=lector.lecobi</td></tr></table>

LEFT JOIN</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT multas.com.c</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT multas.com.c</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT multas.com.c</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT multas</td></tr></table>

word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>copias</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>reserv.reres = sucurs.sucosu</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>coptop</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>copias.cococp = coptip.cpcocp</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>titulo</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>reserv.rentit = titulo.tintit</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT resris.*, lectip.*, copias.*, coptop.*, titulo.*, sucurs.* FROM (((RESHIS INNER JOIN COPIAS ON RESHIS.rhbarc = copias.cobarc) INNER JOIN TITULO ON RESHIS.rhntit = TITULO.tintit) LEFT JOIN LECTIP ON LECTIP = TITULO.pcop) LEFT JOIN COPTIP ON RESHIS.rhcoop = COPTIP.pcop) LEFT JOIN SUCURS ON RESHIS.rhresu = SUCURS.sucosu</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>lectip</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>reshis.rhntit = titulo.tintit</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>copias</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>reshis.rhcolp = lectip.pcop</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>coptop</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>reshis.rhcoop = coptop.cpcop</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>sucurs</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>reserv.rchresu = tulto.tintit</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'></td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>titulo</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>scolec.sccosu = sucurs.sucosu</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>((((((((((((scolec INNER JOIN titulo ON scolec.scntit = titulo.tintit)) INNER JOIN sucurs ON scolec.sccosu = sucurs.sucosu)) LEFT JOIN coploc ON scolec.sccocl = coploc.clcocl)) LEFT JOIN coptop ON coptop ON succul.sccoop = coptop.cpcop)) LEFT JOIN copost ON copsc.sccoce = copost.cecoce) LEFT JOIN provoe ON scolec.scnprv = provoe.pvnprv) LEFT JOIN cuenta ON scolec.sccoou = cuenta.cucocu) LEFT JOIN moneda ON scolec.sccoo = scoco1.o1coo1 = scoco1.coco1) LEFT JOIN prcoed ON prcoed.sccoop = prcoed.pcop)) LEFT JOIN</td><td style='text-align: center; <table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>t_serlec</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>serlec</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>slnlec=lenlec</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT serlec.*,lector.*,scolec.*,titulo.* FROM (((serlec INNER JOIN lector ON slnlec=lenlec) INNER JOIN scolec ON slscsq=scnseq) INNER JOIN titulo ON scnit=tintit) LEFT JOIN scoco1 ON scoco1.scoco1=scoco1.o1co01 ON scoco1.scoco1=scoco2.o2co02</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT ON scolec.sccoo1.scoco1.o1co01) LEFT JOIN scoco2 ON scolec.sccoo2=scoco2.o2co02</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT t_servco.servco.*, scolec.*, provee.*, cuenta.*, moneda.*,scoco1.*,scoco2.* FROM (((servco INNER JOIN provee ON servco.svnpr = provee.pvnprv) INNER JOIN scolec ON servco.svscsq = scolec.scnseq) LEFT JOIN cuenta ON servco.scoco1 = cuenta.cucocu) LEFT JOIN moneda ON scoco1.scoco1=scoco2.o2co02</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>SELECT t_serlec.t_serlec.*, t_servco.servco.*, t_servco.*, t_servco.*, t_</td></tr></table>



<table border="1" style="margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;"><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_copias_presta</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">copias presta</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">messag usrmsg</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">messag.msnseq = usrmsg.ummsns</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT messag.*, usrmsg.* FROM messag LEFT JOIN usrmsg ON messag.msnseq = usrmsg.ummsns</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">userca biblio</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">userca.ususad = usuari.uuusid</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT userca.*,biblio.*,usuari.* FROM ((userca LEFT JOIN usuari ON userca.ususad = usuari.uuusid) left join biblio ON uucobi = bicobi)</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_biblio</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">biblio bibgro</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">biblio.bicobg = bibgro.bgcobg</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT biblio.*,bibgro.bgcobg. &amp;= bibgro.bcog = bibgro.bcog(1,2,FROM ((biblio LEFT ON bibgro ON biblio.bicobg = bibgro.bcog) LEFT JOIN biblio.bicobg1 = bibgro.bcog1) LEFT JOIN biblo2 ON biblio.bicobg2 = bibgro.bcog2)</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_sucurs</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">sucurs biblio</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">sucurs.sucobi = biblio.bicobi</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT sucurs.*,sucro1.*,sucro2.*,bibgro.bcog1.*,bibgro.bcog2.*,bibgro.bcog2.b2cob2</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_sucres</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">sucres biblio</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">sucurs.sucos1 = sucro1.s1cos1</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">sucurs.sucros1 = sucro1.s1cos1</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">sucros.sucros1 = sucro1.sucros1.sucros.sucros1.sucros.sucros2.sucros2.sucros.sucros2.sucros</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_weblkl</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">weblkl\nlector</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">weblkl.wlnlec=lector.lenlec</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT weblkl.*,lector.* FROM weblkl,lector WHERE wlnlec=lenlec</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_lecom</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">lecom\nlector</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">lecom.lcnlec=lector.lenlec</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT lecom.*,lector.* FROM lecom,lector WHERE lcnlec=lenlec</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_z1busq</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">z1busq</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;"></td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT z1busq.* FROM z1busq</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_z2term</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">z2term</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;"></td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT z2term.* FROM z2term</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_z3enla</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">z3enla</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;"></td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT z3enla.* FROM z3enla</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_recurs</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">recurs\nrectip\nsucurs</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">recurs.rccort=rectip.r1cort\nsucurs.sucosu=recurs.rccosu</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT recurs.*, rectip.*, sucurs.* FROM recurs, rectip, sucurs WHERE rccort=r1cort AND sucosu=rccosu</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_recpol</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">recpol\nrectip\nlectip\ncalendar\nhorari</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">recpol.rpcocd=calendar.cdcocd\nrecpol.rpcoho=horari.hocoho\nrecpol.rpcort=rectip.r1cort\nrecpol.rpcolp=lectip.lpcolp</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT recpol.*, rectip.*, lectip.*, calendar.*, horari.* FROM recpol.left.JOIN.calendar ON recpol.rpcocd=calendar.cdcocd\nLEFT JOIN horari ON recpol.rpcoho=horari.hocoho, rectip, lectip WHERE rcport=r1cort AND rpolp=lpcolp</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_rectip</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">rectip</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;"></td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT rectip.* FROM rectip</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_recpre</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">recpre\nrecurs\nlector\nlectip\nrecpol</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">recpre.rsname=recurs.rcname\nrecurs.rccort=rectip.r1cort\nrecpre.rsnlec=lector.lenlec\nrecpre.rscolp=lectip.lpcolp\nrecpre.rsrpns=recpol.rpnseq</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT recpre.*, recurs.*, rectip.*, lectip.*, lector.*, lectip.*, recpol.* FROM recpre, recurs, rectip, lector, lecop, recpol WHERE rsname=rcname AND rcport=r1cort AND rsnlec=lenlec AND rscolp=lpcolp AND rsrpns=rpnseq</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_rechis</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">rechis\nrecurs\nrectip\nlector\nlectip\nrecpol</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">rechis.rxnlec=lector.lenlec\nrechis.rxname=recurs.rcname\nrecurs.rccort=rectip.r1cort\nrechis.rxcolp=lectip.lpcolp\nrechis.rxrpns=recpol.rpnseq</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT rechis.*, recurs.*, rectip.*, lector.*, lectip.*, recpol.* FROM rechis LEFT JOIN LECTOR ON rechis.rxnlec=lector.lenlec, recurs, rectip, lecop, recpol WHERE rxname=rcname AND rcport=r1cort AND rpolp=lpcolp AND rxrpns=rpnseq</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_coptitcop</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">copias\ntitcop\ntitulo</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">copias.cobarc=tcntit.tcbarc\ncopias.contit=titulo.tinit</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT copias.*,tcntit,tititu,tiauto FROM copias,titcop,titulo WHERE cobarc=tcbarc AND contit=tinit</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_copias_titcop</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_coptitcop\ncopnot\nbiblio\nsucurs\ncoploc\ncoptip\ncopest\nproced\ncopsop\ncopco1</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_coptitcop.cobarc = copnot.cnbarc\nt_coptitcop.cocopr = proced.pccopr\nt_coptitcop.cocosu = sucurs.sucosu\nsucurs.sucobi = biblio.bicobi\nt_coptitcop.cococl = coploc.clcocl\nt_coptitcop.cococp = coptip.cpcocp\nt_coptitcop.cococo = coptest.cecoce\nt_coptitcop.cococs = copsop.cscocs\nt_coptitcop.cococ1 = copco1.c1coc1</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT t_coptitcop.*, copnot.*, biblio.*, sucurs.*, coploc.*, coptip.*, copest.*, proced.*, copsoip.*, copco1.* FROM (((((((((t_coptitcop LEFT JOIN copnot ON t_coptitcop.cobarc = copnot.cnbarc) LEFT JOIN proced ON t_coptitcop.cocopr = proced.pccopr) LEFT JOIN sucurs ON t_coptitcop.cocosu = sucurs.sucosu) LEFT JOIN biblio ON sucurs.sucobi = biblio.bicobi) LEFT JOIN coploc ON t_coptitcop.cococl = coploc.clcocl) LEFT JOIN coptip ON t_coptitcop.cococp = coptip.cpcocp) LEFT JOIN copest ON t_coptitcop.cococo = copest.cecoce) LEFT JOIN copsoop ON t_coptitcop.cococs = copsop.cscocs) LEFT JOIN copco1 ON t_coptitcop.cococ1 = copco1.c1coc1</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_prestahis</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">prehis</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;"></td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT PRBARC,PRFSAD,PRUSAD,PRFSMD,PRUSMD,PRPPSQ,PRPRS U,PRCLAS,PRORPR,PRCOSU,PRCOCP,PRCOCL,PRCOCE,PRCO C1,PRNLEC,PRCOLP, PRCOL1,PRCOL2,PRCOL3,PRADUL,PRLEBI,PRLESU,PRFPRE,PRFDEV,PRFULT,PRRECL,PRNREN,PRCORN,PRFRES,PRREPR,PRCOPT,PRCOMP,PRNOTA,PRFORC,PRCOCS,PRFNAC FROM presta UNION PHBARC,PHFSAD,PHUSAD,PHFSMD,PHUSMD,PHNSEQ,PHPR SU,PHCLAS,PHORPR,PHCOSU,PHCOCP,PHCOCL,PHCOCE,PH COC1,-1,PHCOLP, PHCOL1,PHCOL2,PHCOL3,PHADUL,PHLEBI,PHLESU,PHFPRE, PHDEV,PHFULT,PHORPH,PHNREN,PHCORN,PHFULT,PHDISO, PHCOPT,PHCOMP,PHPHSU,PHFORC,PHCOCS,PHFNAC FROM prehis</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_cattip</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">cattip catfld catcod</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">cattip.cinseq = catcod.cktipocatcod.cknseq = codfld.cxcstsqcodfld.cxcfsq = catfld.ccnseq</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT DISTINCT cattip.*, catfld.* FROM cattip left JOIN catcod ON cattip.cinseq = catcod.cktipoleft JOIN codfld ON catcod.cknseq = codfld.cxctsq left JOIN catfld ON codfld.cxcfsq = catfld.ccnseq</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_codfld</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">codfld\ncattipcatfldcatcod</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">cattip.cinseq = catcod.cktipcatcod.cknseq = codfld.cxctsqcodfld.cxcfsq = catfld.ccnseq</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT DISTINCT codfld.*, cattip.*, catfld.* FROM cattip left\nJOIN catcod ON cattip.cinseq = catcod.cktipo left JOIN codfld ON catcod.cknseq = codfld.cxctsq left JOIN catfld ON codfld.cxcfsq = catfld.ccnseq</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_etdesc</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">etdesc\netleti</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">etdesc.eqetsq=etleti.etnseq</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT etdesc.*,etleti.* from etdesc,etleti WHERE eqetsq=etnseq</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_holdin</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">holdin\nbiblio\nsucurs</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">holdin.hhcosu = sucurs.sucosu\nsucurs.sucobi = biblio.bicobi</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT holdin.*, biblio.*, sucurs.* FROM ((holdin LEFT JOIN sucurs ON holdin.hhcosu = sucurs.sucosu) LEFT JOIN biblio ON sucurs.sucobi = biblio.bicobi)</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_weblkb</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">weblkb\nbiblio</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">weblkb.wbcobi=biblio.bicobi</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT * from weblkb,biblio WHERE wbcobi=bicobi</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_tiview</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">tiview\ntitulo</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">tiview.tvntit=titulo.tintit</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">select * from tiview,titulo WHERE tvntit=tintit</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_adqdet_aux</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">adqdet\nadquis\ntitulo\nprovee\nadqlec\nlector cuenta</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">adqdet.adnadq = adquis.aanadq\nadqdet.adnseq = adqlec.aladsq\nadqlec.alnlec = lector.lenlec\nadquis.aanprv = provee.pvnprv\nadquis.aacocu = cuenta.cucocu\nadqdet.adntit = titulo.tintit</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT adqdet.*,adquis.*,titulo.*,provee.*,adqlec.*,lector.*,cuenta.*\nFROM (((ADQDET LEFT JOIN ADQUIS ON ADQDET.adnadq = ADQUIS.aanadq) LEFT JOIN ADQLEC ON ADQDET.adnseq = ADQLEC.aladsq) LEFT JOIN LECTOR ON ADQLEC.alnlec = LECTOR.lenlec) LEFT JOIN PROVEE ON ADQUIS.aanprv = PROVEE.pvnprv) LEFT JOIN CUENTA ON ADQUIS.aacocu = CUENTA.cucocu) INNER JOIN TITULO ON ADQDET.adntit = TITULO.tintit</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_uhpass</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">uhpass</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;"></td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT * FROM uhpass</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_maspre</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">maspre\ntitulo</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">maspre.mantit=titulo.tintit</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT * FROM maspre,titulo WHERE mantit=tintit</td></tr><tr><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">t_masaux</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">masaux</td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;"></td><td style="text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;">SELECT * FROM masaux</td></tr></table>

##### TABLAS RELACIONADAS CON LA LISTA DE MARCADOS

MCNSEQ

<div style="text-align: center;"><img src="https://pplines-online.bj.bcebos.com/deploy/official/paddleocr/pp-ocr-vl-16-online//091f6f8a-634f-4686-92fd-a0025e83c926/markdown_0/imgs/img_in_image_box_481_717_605_808.jpg?authorization=bce-auth-v1%2FALTAKDN8mY5KlNI7zaRpLmOqrw%2F2026-09-16T11%3A24%3A48Z%2F-1%2F%2F49a941e0710ce6ba625a38ff0a306b96f200a479f300af598bc307c1cf058ca7" alt="Image" width="10%" /></div>


TNMCLS
